- **writing**: Orwell's rules from 'Politics and the English Language', applied to prose. Cuts stale metaphors, long words, padding, the passive and jargon. Bans em dashes and semicolons outright and allows a colon only before a list. Also fixes the shape that gives machine-written prose away once the diction is clean: uniform sentence length, resultative tails, clefts, copula-only paragraphs, and shorthand leaking in from working notes.
